" 1 . - A Procedure on employment of a person who 
yay previously a subscriber to any Provident Fund 
of Mie Government or a Body Corporate , owned or 
controlled by the Government : 


By Order 

V . SIVARAMAN 
Regional Director & Ex-OHicio Member- Secretary ,

INDIAN AIRLINES EMPLOYEES PROVIDENT 

TUND NEW DELHI 


Where a person employed in the Corporation was 
previously a subscriber to any Provident Fund of the 
Government or a Hody Corporate , owned or controlled 
by the Government, the transfer of his provident fund 
accumulations from the Governnient Department or the 
body corporate , namely , his subscriptions as also the 
contribulions of the previous employer, if any, together 
with interest thereon , shall be accepted as an initial 
deposit in his account in the Fund . Such a person will 
be periuitted to become i nienber from the date of 
commencement of his employment in the Corporation 
or from the cate of his resignation from the service of 
be Goveruucnt or body corporate owned or controlled 
by the Government takes effect, whichever is later. The 
Corporation s contribution will also be admissible to 
such an cmployec from the date his membership takes 
cffect : 


Now Delhi, the 2011 April 1971
